\"ldh\",\n583\t\t\tErr:   \"starts with hyphen\",\n584\t\t}\n585\t\tassert.Equal(t, `domain \"bad input\": ldh (starts with hyphen)`, err.Error())\n586\t}\n587\t\n588\t// FuzzValidateDomain fuzzes domain validation with random inputs.\n589\t// Properties verified:\n590\t//   - Never panics on any input\n591\t//   - Valid outputs always contain at least one dot (eTLD+1)\n592\t//   - Valid outputs have labels ≤63 characters\n593\t//   - Valid outputs have total length ≤253 characters\n594\tfunc FuzzValidateDomain(f *testing.F) {\n595\t\t// Seed corpus: 9 diverse inputs covering valid and invalid cases\n596\t\tseeds := []string{\n597\t\t\t\"example.com\",           // #1: Simple valid domain\n598\t\t\t\"EXAMPLE.COM\",           // #2: Uppercase (normalizes)\n599\t\t\t\"www.example.co.uk\",     // #3: Multi-level TLD with subdomain\n600\t\t\t\"münchen.de\",            // #4: IDN input (converts to punycode)\n601\t\t\t\"\",                      // #5: Empty string\n602\t\t\t\"example..com\",          // #6: Empty label\n603\t\t\t\"-example.com\",          // #7: Leading hyphen\n604\t\t\t\"http://example.com\",    // #8: URL scheme\n605\t\t\tstrings.Repeat(\"a\", 64) + \".com\", // #9: Label too long (64 chars)\n606\t\t}\n607\t\tfor _, seed := range seeds {\n608\t\t\tf.Add(seed)\n609\t\t}\n610\t\n611\t\tf.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, input string) {\n612\t\t\t// Property 1: Must never panic (the test framework handles this)\n613\t\t\tresult, err := Parse(input)\n614\t\n615\t\t\t// If parsing succeeded, verify invariants\n616\t\t\tif err == nil && result != nil {\n617\t\t\t\t// Property 2: Valid domains contain at least one dot\n618\t\t\t\tif !strings.Contains(result.Domain, \".\") {\n619\t\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"valid domain missing dot: %q\", result.Domain)\n620\t\t\t\t}\n621\t\n622\t\t\t\t// Property 3: Each label ≤63 characters\n623\t\t\t\tlabels := strings.Split(result.Domain, \".\")\n624\t\t\t\tfor i, label := range labels {\n625\t\t\t\t\tif len(label) > 63 {\n626\t\t\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"label %d exceeds 63 chars (%d): %q\", i+1, len(label), label)\n627\t\t\t\t\t}\n628\t\t\t\t}\n629\t\n630\t\t\t\t// Property 4: Total domain length ≤253 characters\n631\t\t\t\tif len(result.Domain) > 253 {\n632\t\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"domain exceeds 253 chars cases per plan.md (#45-48)\nfunc TestParse_EdgeCases(t *testing.T) {\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname        string\n\t\tinput       string\n\t\twantErr     bool\n\t\twantPhase   string // if error expected\n\t\twantDomain  string // if success expected\n\t\tdescription string\n\t}{\n\t\t// #45: PSL private suffix — accepted during parsing\n\t\t// The PSL returns icann=false for private suffixes like github.io.\n\t\t// Parse() accepts these domains; they flow through to RDAP/WHOIS lookup\n\t\t// and are rejected downstream as unsupported_tld if no server covers the TLD.\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"PSL private suffix (github.io)\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"example.github.io\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     false,\n\t\t\twantDomain:  \"example.github.io\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Private PSL suffixes are accepted at parse time, rejected later if TLD unsupported\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t// #46: Wildcard - rejected as invalid character\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"wildcard character\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"*.example.com\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     true,\n\t\t\twantPhase:   \"idna\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Wildcard is not a valid domain character\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t// #47: Invalid punycode - passes validation, RDAP determines availability\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"invalid punycode (syntactically valid)\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"xn--invalid.com\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     false,\n\t\t\twantDomain:  \"xn--invalid.com\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Syntactically valid punycode passes, RDAP returns 404/400\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t// #48: Reserved name - passes validation, check normally\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"reserved name (test.com)\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"test.com\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     false,\n\t\t\twantDomain:  \"test.com\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Reserved names pass validation, registry handles them\",\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tresult, err := Parse(tt.input)\n\t\t\tif tt.wantErr {\n\t\t\t\trequire.Error(t, err, tt.description)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trequire.NoError(t, err, tt.description)\n\t\t\t\tassert.Equal(t, tt.wantDomain, result.Domain, tt.description)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n// Both flow to the existing\n// unsupported_tld path at RDAP/WHOIS check time.\nfunc TestParse_PrivateSuffixAccepted(t *testing.T) {\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname       string\n\t\tinput      string\n\t\twantErr    bool\n\t\twantPhase  string\n\t\twantErrStr string // substring expected in error message\n\t}{\n\t\t// Domains under known PSL private suffixes — must be ACCEPTED during parse\n\t\t{\"github.io\", \"example.github.io\", false, \"\", \"\"},\n\t\t{\"appspot.com\", \"myapp.appspot.com\", false, \"\", \"\"},\n\t\t{\"herokuapp.com\", \"myapp.herokuapp.com\", false, \"\", \"\"},\n\t\t{\"readthedocs.io\", \"docs.readthedocs.io\", false, \"\", \"\"},\n\t\t{\"subdomain under private\", \"deep.sub.github.io\", false, \"\", \"\"},\n\n\t\t// Unknown TLDs (not in PSL at all) — must still pass parse validation.\n\t\t// They will fail later at check time as unsupported_tld.\n\t\t{\"unknown single-label TLD\", \"example.invalidtld\", false, \"\", \"\"},\n\t\t{\"unknown \"example.co.uk\", false, \"\", \"\"},\n\t}\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tresult, err := Parse(tt.input)\n\t\t\tif tt.wantErr {\n\t\t\t\trequire.Error(t, err)\n\t\t\t\trequire.IsType(t, &ParseError{}, err)\n\t\t\t\tpe := err.(*ParseError)\n\t\t\t\tassert.Equal(t, tt.wantPhase, pe.Phase)\n\t\t\t\tassert.Contains(t, pe.Err, tt.wantErrStr)\n\t\t\t\tassert.Nil(t, result)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t\t\t\tassert.NotNil(t, result)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParse_ParseError_Error(t *testing.T) {\n\terr := &ParseError{\n\t\tInput: \"bad input\",\n\t\tPhase: \"ldh\",\n\t\tErr:   \"starts with hyphen\",\n\t}\n\tassert.Equal(t, `domain \"bad input\": ldh (starts with hyphen)`, err.Error())\n}\n\n// FuzzValidateDomain fuzzes domain validation with random inputs.\n// Properties verified:\n//   - Never panics on any input\n//   - Valid outputs always contain at least one dot // #3: Multi-level TLD with subdomain\n\t\t\"münchen.de\",            // #4: IDN input (converts to punycode)\n\t\t\"\",                      // #5: Empty string\n\t\t\"example..com\",          // #6: Empty label\n\t\t\"-example.com\",          // #7: Leading hyphen\n\t\t\"http://example.com\",    // #8: URL scheme\n\t\tstrings.Repeat(\"a\", 64) + \".com\", // #9: Label too long (64 chars)\n\t}\n\tfor _, seed := range seeds {\n\t\tf.Add(seed)\n\t}\n\n\tf.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, input string) {\n\t\t// Property 1: Must never panic (the test framework handles this)\n\t\tresult, err result.Domain)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n\nfunc TestParse_EffectiveTLDPlusOne(t *testing.T) {\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname  string\n\t\tinput string\n\t\twant  string\n\t}{\n\t\t{name: \"com\", cases per plan.md (#45-48)\nfunc TestParse_EdgeCases(t *testing.T) {\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname        string\n\t\tinput       string\n\t\twantErr     bool\n\t\twantPhase   string // if error expected\n\t\twantDomain  string // if success expected\n\t\tdescription string\n\t}{\n\t\t// #45: PSL private suffix — accepted during parsing\n\t\t// The PSL returns icann=false for private suffixes like github.io.\n\t\t// Parse() accepts these domains; they flow through to RDAP/WHOIS lookup\n\t\t// and are rejected downstream as unsupported_tld if no server covers the TLD.\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"PSL private suffix (github.io)\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"example.github.io\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     false,\n\t\t\twantDomain:  \"example.github.io\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Private PSL suffixes are accepted at parse time, rejected later if TLD unsupported\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t// #46: Wildcard - rejected as invalid character\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"wildcard character\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"*.example.com\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     true,\n\t\t\twantPhase:   \"idna\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Wildcard is not a valid domain character\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t// #47: Invalid punycode - passes validation, RDAP determines availability\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"invalid punycode (syntactically valid)\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"xn--invalid.com\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     false,\n\t\t\twantDomain:  \"xn--invalid.com\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Syntactically valid punycode passes, RDAP returns 404/400\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t// #48: Reserved name - passes validation, check normally\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:        \"reserved name (test.com)\",\n\t\t\tinput:       \"test.com\",\n\t\t\twantErr:     false,\n\t\t\twantDomain:  \"test.com\",\n\t\t\tdescription: \"Reserved names pass validation, registry handles them\",\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tresult, err := Parse(tt.input)\n\t\t\tif tt.wantErr {\n\t\t\t\trequire.Error(t, err, tt.description)\n\t\t\t\tif tt.wantPhase != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\trequire.IsType(t, &ParseError{}, err)\n\t\t\t\t\tpe := err.(*ParseError)\n\t\t\t\t\tassert.Equal(t, tt.wantPhase, pe.Phase, tt.description)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trequire.NoError(t, err, tt.description)\n\t\t\t\tassert.Equal(t, tt.wantDomain, result.Domain, tt.description)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n//
